Second transcription's note: Treats eligible persons entered in this collection as persons the filer claims. No separate election to leave an eligible person unclaimed is supplied.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement, "two or more persons, each of whom would be able to claim the person as a dependent but for the support test" (a qualifying-relative rule; the project refuses dependent.multiple_support only on its qualifying-relative test); p501 p. 18, Kidnapped child. executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.gpt.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 8,522 of 9,011 agree; 213 candidate-unknown, 109 project-refuses, 83 project-unknown, 73 value, 11 candidate-refuses; on completed returns: 83 candidate-unknown, 11 value, 2 candidate-refuses. It requires dependent.joint_return no for every person, which the project asks only of a married person (29 completed unknowns), and asks dependent.lawful where the relationship answer settles it (3). Every completed value (11, project yes and candidate no) is married no with joint_return yes, a record the interview cannot produce: dependent.married asks "married at the end of 2025 ..., or is a joint return being filed for 2025 that includes this person", so a No settles the joint return. It refuses every kidnapped child (h_kidnap_review: 29 completed unknowns), which the project computes under its kidnapping gates (owner decision 3 of 2026-11-03), and its multiple-support refusal reaches qualifying children. Project stands.

Any person who is your dependent only because the person lived with you for all of 2025 ... 2. Your unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent"; p501 p. 9, Temporary absences, "It must be reasonable to assume the absent person will return to the home after the temporary absence". executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.gpt.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 8,873 of 9,011 agree; 122 project-refuses, 10 candidate-refuses, 5 project-unknown, 1 candidate-unknown; on completed returns: 2 candidate-refuses, 1 candidate-unknown. Its gate (a Test 2 candidate with absence_no_return) is the project's design; differences are refusals (project 122, candidate multiple-support 10) and 1 completed unknown on a partial record. No value. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return". executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.gpt.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 8,744 of 9,011 agree; 233 candidate-refuses, 28 candidate-unknown, 4 project-refuses, 2 value; on completed returns: 4 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses, 1 value. Its American opportunity test requires dependent.edu_tin_issued where the project accepts a timely ITIN or ATIN application: classm.f8863.2 and classm.aqee.1 (and .deepseek variants) are completed returns where the project gives line 27 $4,000.00 and the candidate needs the answer (4 completed unknowns). The completed value (generated 445 dep_a, $1,471.34 against $0.00) is married no with joint_return yes, a record the interview cannot produce: dependent.married asks "married at the end of 2025 ..., or is a joint return being filed for 2025 that includes this person", so a No settles the joint return. Project stands.

### 28 — Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-
Status: **DISAGREE**

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; i8863 p. 9, Line 31. executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.gpt.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 8,738 of 9,011 agree; 233 candidate-refuses, 34 candidate-unknown, 5 project-refuses, 1 project-unknown; on completed returns: 4 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. Its American opportunity test requires dependent.edu_tin_issued where the project accepts a timely ITIN or ATIN application: classm.f8863.2 and classm.aqee.1 (and .deepseek variants) are completed returns where the project gives an American opportunity amount and the candidate needs the answer (4 completed unknowns). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.dc_paid
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_dc_allocation]
    cite: "Form 2441 (2025), page 1, line 2(d); instructions, pages 3–4."
    note: "The interface does not allocate Form 2441 line 28 benefits among persons. A positive excluded or deducted benefit amount requires review rather than an invented per-person allocation."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 2441 (2025), page 3, line 2, column (c)."
    note: "Age is tested when care was provided. For someone age 13 at year end, the supplied care-period answer is necessary; incapacity alone does not establish that any included care occurred after the thirteenth birthday."

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don’t enter more than $4,000."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_edu_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 27; instructions, page 9."

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {param: aoc.full_band}}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 28."

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 29."

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}
          then: dependent.edu_line27
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}
          then: dependent.h_edu_plus
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 30."

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions)."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_edu_net_inst
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 31; instructions, pages 4 and 9."

  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Each dependent"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8962 (2025), page 8, tax family size."
    note: "One per person; consuming totals must filter for the applicable qualification."

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support, dependent.married, dependent.h_competing, dependent.h_kidnap_review]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 17–22, Steps 1, 2 and 4."
    note: "Treats eligible persons entered in this collection as persons the filer claims. No separate election to leave an eligible person unclaimed is supplied."

  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying children under age 17 with the required social security number"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 17–18 and 22; Schedule 8812 (2025), line 4."

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Other dependents, including any qualifying children who are not under age 17 or who do not have the required social security number"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 18–22, Steps 3 and 5; Schedule 8812, line 6."
    note: "Uses the issued-TIN requirement stated on pages 17 and 22. Step 5 also permits timely applications that result in issuance; the published household.all_tin interface must account for that treatment."

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 4 by $2,200."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_ctc_unit
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    cite: "Schedule 8812 (2025), page 1, line 5."
    note: "Per-person contribution before return-level phaseout and credit limits."

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 6 by $500."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_odc_unit
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    cite: "Schedule 8812 (2025), page 1, line 7."
    note: "Per-person contribution before return-level phaseout and credit limits."

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children under age 17 with the required social security number: × $1,700."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_actc_unit
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    cite: "Schedule 8812 (2025), page 2, line 16b."
    note: "Per-person contribution to line 16b, not the final additional child tax credit."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "It must be reasonable to assume the absent person will return to the home after the temporary absence."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_hoh_candidate, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Publication 501 (2025), page 9, Temporary absences."
    note: "Flags a potentially disqualifying absence. The interface does not supply residence time with that absence removed."

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.h_hoh_candidate}
    refuses: [dependent.hoh_absence, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support, dependent.married, dependent.h_competing, dependent.h_kidnap_review]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 14–15; Publication 501 (2025), page 9."
    note: "Per-person qualification; the return separately tests payment of more than half the home's cost and unmarried status."

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), page 14, Test 1; Publication 501 (2025), page 9."

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_child_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_child_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.h_competing, dependent.h_eic_kidnap_review]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 41 and 43–45."
    note: "No support test or dependency requirement is imposed for an unmarried EIC qualifying child."

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 44–46."

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_young, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_residence, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 2441 (2025), page 1, Qualifying Person(s); Publication 503 (2025), page 3."
    note: "This dependent-entry slice computes claimable dependents. Persons qualifying only under the gross-income, joint-return or filer-dependency exceptions are addressed by the supplied Form 2441 nondependent screening interface. Qualification does not require positive care expenses."

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Each qualifying person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dc.one}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 2441 (2025), page 4, qualifying-person count."
    note: "A unit count, to be aggregated with a filter on dependent.dc_qp. The grammar cannot choose between Count values; this is not an unfiltered eligibility indicator."

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Modified AGI for your dependents who are required to file an income tax return"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8962 (2025), page 8, line 2b and Worksheet 1-2."

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 28."

  - id: dependent.h_child_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 17 and 41."

  - id: dependent.h_child_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19; a full-time student under age 24; or permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
                - op: test
                  all:
                    - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 17 and 41."

  - id: dependent.h_half_year
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 17 and 21."
    note: "Uses the supplied months encoding, including its birth, death, adoption and placement rules."

  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_child_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_child_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_half_year, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), page 17, Step 1."

  - id: dependent.h_relative
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relatives who don’t have to live with you"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Publication 501 (2025), page 19, Member of Household or Relationship Test."

  - id: dependent.h_qr_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of household or relationship test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_relative, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Publication 501 (2025), page 19."

  - id: dependent.h_competing
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child of more than one person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 21–22 and 44; Publication 501 (2025), page 18."
    note: "The closed interface lacks the other claimants' parentage, residence comparisons, AGIs and claim choices. Competing taxpayer claims require review."

  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.h_sheltered_review]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), page 19; Publication 501 (2025), pages 18–19."

  - id: dependent.h_sheltered_review
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Disabled dependent working at sheltered workshop"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
    cite: "Publication 501 (2025), page 19, Gross Income Test."
    note: "No sheltered-workshop income amount is supplied. A potentially outcome-changing exclusion requires review."

  - id: dependent.h_kidnap_review
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Kidnapped child"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Publication 501 (2025), pages 13 and 18."
    note: "Pre-kidnapping and post-return residence or support facts and termination conditions are not supplied; no kidnapped-child exception is computed."

  - id: dependent.h_eic_kidnap_review
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception to time lived with you"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: false}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), pages 21 and 43."
    note: "The supplied snapshots refer EIC kidnapping details to Publication 596; those details and the necessary facts are not supplied."

  - id: dependent.h_hoh_residence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception to time lived with you"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_half_year, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.placed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), page 15; Publication 501 (2025), page 9."

  - id: dependent.h_hoh_candidate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any person whom you can claim as a dependent; your unmarried qualifying child who isn’t your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_hoh_residence, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_relative, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 1040 (2025), page 14, Test 2."

  - id: dependent.h_dc_young
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 when the care was provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Publication 503 (2025), page 3; Instructions for Form 2441 (2025), page 1."

  - id: dependent.h_dc_residence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_half_year, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Publication 503 (2025), page 3, Birth or death of otherwise qualifying person."

  - id: dependent.h_dc_allocation
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Don’t include in column (d) any benefits shown on line 28."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f2441.benefits, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f2441.line28, {const: 0}]}
    cite: "Form 2441 (2025), page 2, line 30; instructions, page 3, column (d)."
    note: "A positive result requires benefit allocation information absent from the closed interface."

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_unit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}
    cite: "Schedule 8812 (2025), line 5."

  - id: dependent.h_odc_unit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}
    cite: "Schedule 8812 (2025), line 7."

  - id: dependent.h_actc_unit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}
    cite: "Schedule 8812 (2025), line 16b."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_student
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A dependent you claim on your tax return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8863.eligible, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8863 (2025), pages 1–3."
    note: "Uses the issued-by-due-date reading in the Form 8863 instructions. Publication 970 page 11 also treats a timely application resulting in issuance as timely issuance; the closed input does not identify that subsequent issuance."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student qualifications"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_student, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8863 (2025), pages 3–4 and 8."
    note: "Selects American opportunity credit when eligible, following the page 3 tip; return-level disallowance screening and phaseout remain on Form 8863."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You may be able to claim the lifetime learning credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_student, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8863 (2025), page 4; Form 8863, page 2."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adjustments
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments (add lines 2a, 2b, and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8863 (2025), page 9, Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet, line 3."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_net_inst
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adjustments}
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8863 (2025), pages 4 and 9."
    note: "Annual aggregation is used only when adjustments do not exceed institutional expenses in any academic period. Otherwise the interface lacks period-level amounts needed to apply separate zero floors."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_expenses
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tuition, fees, and course materials"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.h_edu_net_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}
    cite: "Instructions for Form 8863 (2025), page 4, Qualified Education Expenses."
    note: "Within the no-period-excess scope, adding other course materials after subtracting adjustments is equivalent to subtracting adjustments from total qualified expenses."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 27."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Don’t enter more than $4,000."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_expenses, dependent.h_edu_cap]}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 27."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, lines 28 and 30."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.h_edu_band, dependent.edu_line29]}
    cite: "Form 8863 (2025), page 2, line 30."
```
